114 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
115 |     if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
116 |       Var v = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
117 |       if (v != null && varFilter.apply(v)) {
118 |         addReference(t, v,

===== M0011 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ReferenceCollectingCallback.ReferenceCollection.isWellDefined()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ReferenceCollectingCallback.java
Original range: 243-266
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines if the variable for this reference collection is
     * "well-defined." A variable is well-defined if we can prove at
     * compile-time that it's assigned a value before it's used.
     *
     * Notice that if this function returns false, this doesn't imply that the
     * variable is used before it's assigned. It just means that we don't
     * have enough information to make a definitive judgement.
     */
Method code:
243 |     protected boolean isWellDefined() {
244 |       int size = references.size();
245 |       if (size == 0) {
246 |         return false;
247 |       }
248 | 
249 |       // If this is a declaration that does not instantiate the variable,
250 |       // it's not well-defined.
251 |       Reference init = getInitializingReference();
252 |       if (init == null) {
253 |         return false;
254 |       }
255 | 
256 |       Preconditions.checkState(references.get(0).isDeclaration());
257 |       BasicBlock initBlock = init.getBasicBlock();
258 |       for (int i = 1; i < size; i++) {
259 |         if (!initBlock.provablyExecutesBefore(
260 |                 references.get(i).getBasicBlock())) {
261 |           return false;
262 |         }
263 |       }
264 | 
265 |       return true;
266 |     }

99 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
100 |     }

===== M0014 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.removeChild(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1089-1125
Documentation:
/** Safely remove children while maintaining a valid node structure. */
Method code:
1089 |   static void removeChild(Node parent, Node node) {
1090 |     // Node parent = node.getParent();
1091 |     if (isStatementBlock(parent)
1092 |         || isSwitchCase(node)
1093 |         || isTryFinallyNode(parent, node)) {
1094 |       // A statement in a block can simply be removed.
1095 |       parent.removeChild(node);
1096 |     } else if (parent.getType() == Token.VAR) {
1097 |       if (parent.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1098 |         parent.removeChild(node);
1099 |       } else {
1100 |         // Remove the node from the parent, so it can be reused.
1101 |         parent.removeChild(node);
1102 |         // This would leave an empty VAR, remove the VAR itself.
1103 |         removeChild(parent.getParent(), parent);
1104 |       }
1105 |     } else if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK) {
1106 |       // Simply empty the block.  This maintains source location and
1107 |       // "synthetic"-ness.
1108 |       node.detachChildren();
1109 |     } else if (parent.getType() == Token.LABEL
1110 |         && node == parent.getLastChild()) {
1111 |       // Remove the node from the parent, so it can be reused.
1112 |       parent.removeChild(node);
1113 |       // A LABEL without children can not be referred to, remove it.
1114 |       removeChild(parent.getParent(), parent);
1115 |     } else if (parent.getType() == Token.FOR
1116 |         && parent.getChildCount() == 4) {
1117 |       // Only Token.FOR can have an Token.EMPTY other control structure
1118 |       // need something for the condition. Others need to be replaced
1119 |       // or the structure removed.
1120 |       parent.replaceChild(node, new Node(Token.EMPTY));
1121 |     } else {
1122 |       throw new IllegalStateException("Invalid attempt to remove node: " +
1123 |           node.toString() + " of "+ parent.toString());
1124 |     }
1125 |   }

===== M0015 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 995-1122
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
995 |   Node parseInputs() {
996 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
997 | 
998 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
999 |     // individual file parse trees.
1000 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1001 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1002 |     }
1003 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1004 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1005 |     }
1006 | 
1007 |     // Parse main js sources.
1008 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1009 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1010 | 
1011 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1012 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1013 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1014 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1015 |     }
1016 | 
1017 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1018 | 
1019 |     try {
1020 |       // Parse externs sources.
1021 |       externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1022 |       externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1023 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1024 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1025 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1026 |           return null;
1027 |         }
1028 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1029 |       }
1030 | 
1031 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1032 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1033 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1034 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1035 | 
1036 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1037 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1038 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1039 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1040 |           }
1041 |         }
1042 | 
1043 |         try {
1044 |           inputs =
1045 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1046 |               .manageDependencies(inputs);
1047 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1048 |           report(JSError.make(
1049 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1050 |           return null;
1051 |         }
1052 |       }
1053 | 
1054 |       // Check if externs files need to be lifted.
1055 |       boolean liftedExterns = false;
1056 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1057 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1058 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1059 |           return null;
1060 |         }
1061 | 
1062 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1063 |         if (n == null) {
1064 |           continue;
1065 |         }
1066 | 
1067 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null && n.getJSDocInfo().isExterns()) {
1068 |           // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1069 |           // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1070 |           // the externs pile anyways.
1071 |           externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1072 |           input.setIsExtern(true);
1073 | 
1074 |           // TODO(nicksantos): We need a better mechanism to make sure
1075 |           // changes to the inputs array get reflected in JSModules.
1076 |           input.getModule().remove(input);
1077 | 
1078 |           externs.add(input);
1079 |           liftedExterns = true;
1080 |         }
1081 |       }
1082 | 
1083 |       if (liftedExterns) {
1084 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1085 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1086 |       }
1087 | 
1088 |       // Build the AST.
1089 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1090 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1091 |         if (n == null) {
1092 |           continue;
1093 |         }
1094 | 
1095 |         if (devMode) {
1096 |           runSanityCheck();
1097 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1098 |             return null;
1099 |           }
1100 |         }
1101 | 
1102 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1103 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1104 | 
1105 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1106 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1107 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1108 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(input.getName());
1109 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1110 |         }
1111 | 
1112 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1113 |       }
1114 | 
1115 |       externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1116 |       externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1117 | 
1118 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1119 |     } finally {
1120 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1121 |     }
1122 |   }

===== M0018 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ReferenceCollectingCallback.isBlockBoundary(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ReferenceCollectingCallback.java
Original range: 160-190
Documentation:
/**
   * @return true if this node marks the start of a new basic block
   */
Method code:
160 |   private static boolean isBlockBoundary(Node n, Node parent) {
161 |     if (parent != null) {
162 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
163 |         case Token.DO:
164 |         case Token.FOR:
165 |         case Token.TRY:
166 |         case Token.WHILE:
167 |         case Token.WITH:
168 |           // NOTE: TRY has up to 3 child blocks:
169 |           // TRY
170 |           //   BLOCK
171 |           //   BLOCK
172 |           //     CATCH
173 |           //   BLOCK
174 |           // Note that there is an explcit CATCH token but no explicit
175 |           // FINALLY token. For simplicity, we consider each BLOCK
176 |           // a separate basic BLOCK.
177 |           return true;
178 |         case Token.AND:
179 |         case Token.HOOK:
180 |         case Token.IF:
181 |         case Token.OR:
182 |           // The first child of a conditional is not a boundary,
183 |           // but all the rest of the children are.
184 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
185 | 
186 |       }
187 |     }
188 | 
189 |     return n.getType() == Token.CASE;
190 |   }

===== M0026 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.toSource(Node,Format,boolean,int,SourceMap,Charset)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 636-659
Documentation:
/**
   * Converts a tree to js code
   */
Method code:
636 |   private static String toSource(Node root, Format outputFormat,
637 |                                  boolean lineBreak,  int lineLengthThreshold,
638 |                                  SourceMap sourceMap,
639 |                                  Charset outputCharset) {
640 |     boolean createSourceMap = (sourceMap != null);
641 |     CodeConsumer cp =
642 |         outputFormat == Format.COMPACT
643 |         ? new CompactCodePrinter(
644 |             lineBreak, lineLengthThreshold, createSourceMap)
645 |         : new PrettyCodePrinter(lineLengthThreshold, createSourceMap);
646 |     CodeGenerator cg =
647 |         outputFormat == Format.TYPED
648 |         ? new TypedCodeGenerator(cp, outputCharset)
649 |         : new CodeGenerator(cp, outputCharset);
650 |     cg.add(root);
651 | 
652 |     String code = ((HasGetCode) cp).getCode();
653 | 
654 |     if (createSourceMap) {
655 |       cp.generateSourceMap(sourceMap);
656 |     }
657 | 
658 |     return code;
659 |   }

===== M0030 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 151-227
Documentation:
/**
     *
     * In the AST that Rhino gives us, it needs to make a distinction
     * between jsdoc on the object literal node and jsdoc on the object literal
     * value. For example,
     * <pre>
     * var x = {
     *   / JSDOC /
     *   a: 'b',
     *   c: / JSDOC / 'd'
     * };
     * </pre>
     *
     * But in few narrow cases (in particular, function literals), it's
     * a lot easier for us if the doc is attached to the value.
     */
Method code:
151 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
152 |       int nType = n.getType();
153 |       switch (nType) {
154 |         case Token.NAME:
155 |         case Token.STRING:
156 |           String nString = n.getString();
157 |           if (nType == Token.NAME &&
158 |               n.getParent().getType() == Token.CALL &&
159 |               "eval".equals(nString)) {
160 |             n.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
161 |           }
162 |           if (convention.isConstant(nString)) {
163 |             n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
164 |           }
165 |           break;
166 | 
167 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
168 |           JSDocInfo fnInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
169 |           if (fnInfo == null) {
170 |             // Look for the info on other nodes.
171 |             if (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
172 |               // on ASSIGNs
173 |               fnInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
174 |             } else if (parent.getType() == Token.NAME) {
175 |               // on var NAME = function() { ... };
176 |               fnInfo = parent.getParent().getJSDocInfo();
177 |             }
178 |           }
179 | 
180 |           // Compute which function parameters are optional and
181 |           // which are var_args.
182 |           Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
183 |           for (Node arg = args.getFirstChild();
184 |                arg != null;
185 |                arg = arg.getNext()) {
186 |             String argName = arg.getString();
187 |             JSTypeExpression typeExpr = fnInfo == null ?
188 |                 null : fnInfo.getParameterType(argName);
189 | 
190 |             if (convention.isOptionalParameter(arg) ||
191 |                 typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isOptionalArg()) {
192 |               arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_OPTIONAL_PARAM, true);
193 |             }
194 |             if (convention.isVarArgsParameter(arg) ||
195 |                 typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isVarArgs()) {
196 |               arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_VAR_ARGS_PARAM, true);
197 |             }
198 |           }
199 |           break;
200 | 
201 |         case Token.OBJECTLIT:
202 |           if (n.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
203 |             for (Node key = n.getFirstChild();
204 |                  key != null; key = key.getNext().getNext()) {
205 |               Node value = key.getNext();
206 |               if (key.getJSDocInfo() != null &&
207 |                   key.getNext().getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
208 |                 value.setJSDocInfo(key.getJSDocInfo());
209 |               }
210 |             }
211 |           }
212 |           break;
213 |       }
214 | 
215 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Determine if it is possible to simply use the javadoc
216 |       // everywhere rather than use IS_DISPATCHER.
217 |       /*
218 |        * Translate dispatcher info into the property expected node.
219 |        */
220 |       if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null && n.getJSDocInfo().isJavaDispatch()) {
221 |         if (n.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
222 |           Node fnNode = n.getLastChild();
223 |           Preconditions.checkState(fnNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
224 |           fnNode.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_DISPATCHER, true);
225 |         }
226 |       }
227 |     }

276 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {

===== M0040 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 421-447
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
421 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
422 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
423 | 
424 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
425 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
426 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
427 |       return 0;
428 |     }
429 | 
430 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
431 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
432 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than extraTracingStatistics.size() because
433 |       // a new statistic may have been added
434 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
435 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
436 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
437 |       }
438 |     }
439 | 
440 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
441 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
442 |       return 0;
443 |     }
444 | 
445 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
446 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
447 |   }

===== M0042 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 364-379
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
364 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
365 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
366 |       Set<String> directives = null;
367 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
368 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
369 |         if (directives == null) {
370 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
371 |         } else {
372 |           directives.add(directive);
373 |         }
374 |       }
375 | 
376 |       if (directives != null) {
377 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
378 |       }
379 |     }

===== M0045 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ArrowType.hashCode()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ArrowType.java
Original range: 187-207
Documentation:

Method code:
187 |   @Override
188 |   public int hashCode() {
189 |     int hashCode = 0;
190 |     if (returnType != null) {
191 |       hashCode += returnType.hashCode();
192 |     }
193 |     if (returnTypeInferred) {
194 |       hashCode += 1;
195 |     }
196 |     if (parameters != null) {
197 |       Node param = parameters.getFirstChild();
198 |       while (param != null) {
199 |         JSType paramType = param.getJSType();
200 |         if (paramType != null) {
201 |           hashCode += paramType.hashCode();
202 |         }
203 |         param = param.getNext();
204 |       }
205 |     }
206 |     return hashCode;
207 |   }

507 |           }

518 |         }

520 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);

522 |         break;

524 |       case Token.NEW:
525 |         add("new ");
526 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
527 | 
528 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
529 |         // to force parens. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
530 |         // first viable parens
531 |         if (NodeUtil.containsCall(first)) {
532 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
533 |         }
534 |         addExpr(first, precedence);
535 | 
536 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
537 |         Node next = first.getNext();
538 |         if (next != null) {
539 |           add("(");

541 |           add(")");
542 |         }
543 |         break;

547 |         add(jsString(n.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder));
548 |         break;

556 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {

558 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
559 |         if (needsParens) {

561 |         }

564 |           if (c != first) {

566 |           }
567 | 
568 |           // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they are
569 |           // not JavaScript keywords
570 |           if (c.getType() == Token.STRING &&

582 |         }
583 |         add("}");
584 |         if (needsParens) {

586 |         }
587 |         break;
588 |       }
589 | 
590 |       case Token.SWITCH:
591 |         add("switch(");
592 |         add(first);

596 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
597 |         break;

599 |       case Token.CASE:

604 |         break;
605 | 
606 |       case Token.DEFAULT:

608 |         add("default");

610 |         break;
611 | 
612 |       case Token.LABEL:

614 |         if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {

616 |         }

620 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
621 |         break;
622 | 
623 |       // This node is auto generated in anonymous functions and should just get
624 |       // ignored for our purposes.
625 |       case Token.SETNAME:
626 |         break;
627 | 
628 |       default:
629 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
630 |     }
631 | 
632 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
633 |   }

===== M0051 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1615-1625
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
1615 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
1616 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
1617 |     int len = s.length();
1618 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
1619 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
1620 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
1621 |         return false;
1622 |       }
1623 |     }
1624 |     return true;
1625 |   }

Method code:
264 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
265 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
266 |       return;
267 |     }
268 | 
269 |     try {
270 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
271 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
272 | 
273 |       sourceName = "";
274 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
275 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
276 | 
277 |       for (Node root : roots) {
278 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
279 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);
280 |       }
281 | 
282 |       popScope();
283 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {
284 |       throwUnexpectedException(unexpectedException);
285 |     }
286 |   }

===== M0085 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.PropogateConstantAnnotations.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)

Original range: 132-162
Documentation:

Method code:
132 |     @Override
133 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
134 |       // Note: Constant properties annotations are not propagated.
135 |       if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
136 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
137 |           return;
138 |         }
139 | 
140 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
141 |         // Find the JSDocInfo for a top level variable.
142 |         Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
143 |         if (var != null) {
144 |           info = var.getJSDocInfo();
145 |         }
146 | 
147 |         if ((info != null && info.isConstant()) &&
148 |             !n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME)) {
149 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
150 |           if (assertOnChange) {
151 |             String name = n.getString();
152 |             throw new IllegalStateException(
153 |                 "Unexpected const change.\n" +
154 |                 "  name: "+ name + "\n" +
155 |                 "  gramps:" + n.getParent().getParent().toStringTree());
156 |           }
157 |           // Even though the AST has changed (an annotation was added),
158 |           // the annotations are not compared so don't report the change.
159 |           // reportCodeChange("constant annotation");
160 |         }
161 |       }
162 |     }

===== M0091 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineVariables.InliningBehavior.isImmutableAndWellDefinedVariable(Var,ReferenceCollection)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineVariables.java
Original range: 635-685
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines whether the reference collection describes a variable that
     * is initialized to an immutable value, never modified, and defined before
     * every reference.
     */
Method code:
635 |     private boolean isImmutableAndWellDefinedVariable(Var v,
636 |         ReferenceCollection refInfo) {
637 |       List<Reference> refSet = refInfo.references;
638 |       int startingReadRef = 1;
639 |       Reference refDecl = refSet.get(0);
640 |       if (!isValidDeclaration(refDecl)) {
641 |         return false;
642 |       }
643 | 
644 |       boolean isNeverAssigned = refInfo.isNeverAssigned();
645 |       // For values that are never assigned, only the references need to be
646 |       // checked.
647 |       if (!isNeverAssigned) {
648 |         Reference refInit = refInfo.getInitializingReference();
649 |         if (!isValidInitialization(refInit)) {
650 |           return false;
651 |         }
652 | 
653 |         if (refDecl != refInit) {
654 |           Preconditions.checkState(refInit == refSet.get(1));
655 |           startingReadRef = 2;
656 |         }
657 | 
658 |         if (!refInfo.isWellDefined()) {
659 |           return false;
660 |         }
661 | 
662 |         Node value = refInit.getAssignedValue();
663 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(value);
664 | 
665 |         boolean isImmutableValueWorthInlining =
666 |             NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(value) &&
667 |             (value.getType() != Token.STRING ||
668 |                 isStringWorthInlining(v, refInfo.references));
669 |         boolean isInlinableThisAlias =
670 |             value.getType() == Token.THIS &&
671 |             !refInfo.isEscaped();
672 |         if (!isImmutableValueWorthInlining && !isInlinableThisAlias) {
673 |           return false;
674 |         }
675 |       }
676 | 
677 |       for (int i = startingReadRef; i < refSet.size(); i++) {
678 |         Reference ref = refSet.get(i);
679 |         if (!isValidReference(ref)) {
680 |           return false;
681 |         }
682 |       }
683 | 
684 |       return true;
685 |     }

===== M0094 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.opToStr(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1346-1391
Documentation:
/**
   * Converts an operator's token value (see {@link Token}) to a string
   * representation.
   *
   * @param operator the operator's token value to convert
   * @return the string representation or {@code null} if the token value is
   * not an operator
   */
Method code:
1346 |   static String opToStr(int operator) {
1347 |     switch (operator) {
1348 |       case Token.BITOR: return "|";
1349 |       case Token.OR: return "||";
1350 |       case Token.BITXOR: return "^";
1351 |       case Token.AND: return "&&";
1352 |       case Token.BITAND: return "&";
1353 |       case Token.SHEQ: return "===";
1354 |       case Token.EQ: return "==";
1355 |       case Token.NOT: return "!";
1356 |       case Token.NE: return "!=";
1357 |       case Token.SHNE: return "!==";
1358 |       case Token.LSH: return "<<";
1359 |       case Token.IN: return "in";
1360 |       case Token.LE: return "<=";
1361 |       case Token.LT: return "<";
1362 |       case Token.URSH: return ">>>";
1363 |       case Token.RSH: return ">>";
1364 |       case Token.GE: return ">=";
1365 |       case Token.GT: return ">";
1366 |       case Token.MUL: return "*";
1367 |       case Token.DIV: return "/";
1368 |       case Token.MOD: return "%";
1369 |       case Token.BITNOT: return "~";
1370 |       case Token.ADD: return "+";
1371 |       case Token.SUB: return "-";
1372 |       case Token.POS: return "+";
1373 |       case Token.NEG: return "-";
1374 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return "=";

===== M0097 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineVariables.InliningBehavior.isInlineableDeclaredConstant(Var,ReferenceCollection)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineVariables.java
Original range: 422-453
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines whether the given variable is declared as a constant
     * and may be inlined.
     */
Method code:
422 |     private boolean isInlineableDeclaredConstant(Var var,
423 |         ReferenceCollection refInfo) {
424 |       if (!identifyConstants.apply(var)) {
425 |         return false;
426 |       }
427 | 
428 |       if (!refInfo.isAssignedOnceInLifetime()) {
429 |         return false;
430 |       }
431 | 
432 |       Reference init = refInfo.getInitializingReferenceForConstants();
433 |       if (init == null) {
434 |         return false;
435 |       }
436 | 
437 |       Node value = init.getAssignedValue();
438 |       if (value == null) {
439 |         // This constant is either externally defined or initialized indirectly
440 |         // (e.g. in an function expression used to hide
441 |         // temporary variables), so the constant is ineligible for inlining.
442 |         return false;
443 |       }
444 | 
445 |       // Is the constant's value immutable?
446 |       if (!NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(value)) {
447 |         return false;
448 |       }
449 | 
450 |       // Determine if we should really inline a String or not.
451 |       return value.getType() != Token.STRING ||
452 |           isStringWorthInlining(var, refInfo.references);
453 |     }

===== M0105 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1115-1123
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1115 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1116 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1117 |       return -1;
1118 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1119 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1120 |     } else {
1121 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1122 |     }
1123 |   }

===== M0106 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.VerifyConstants.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 191-239
Documentation:

Method code:
191 |     @Override
192 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
193 |       if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
194 |         String name = n.getString();
195 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
196 |           return;
197 |         }
198 | 
199 |         boolean isConst = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
200 |         if (checkUserDeclarations) {
201 |           boolean expectedConst = false;
202 |           if (NodeUtil.isConstantName(n)
203 |               || compiler.getCodingConvention().isConstant(n.getString())) {
204 |             expectedConst = true;
205 |           } else {
206 |             expectedConst = false;
207 | 
208 |             JSDocInfo info = null;
209 |             Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
210 |             if (var != null) {
211 |               info = var.getJSDocInfo();
212 |             }
213 | 
214 |             if (info != null && info.isConstant()) {
215 |               expectedConst = true;
216 |             } else {
217 |               expectedConst = false;
218 |             }
219 |           }
220 | 
221 |           if (expectedConst) {
222 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
223 |                 "The name " + name + " is not annotated as constant.");
224 |           } else {
225 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
226 |                 "The name " + name + " should not be annotated as constant.");
227 |           }
228 |         }
229 | 
230 |         Boolean value = constantMap.get(name);
231 |         if (value == null) {
232 |           constantMap.put(name, isConst);
233 |         } else {
234 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.booleanValue() == isConst,
235 |               "The name " + name + " is not consistently annotated as " +
236 |               "constant.");
237 |         }
238 |       }
239 |     }

/**

===== M0112 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.addNonEmptyExpression(Node,Context,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 642-696
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds a block or expression, substituting a VOID with an empty statement.
   * This is used for "for (...);" and "if (...);" type statements.
   *
   * @param n The node to print.
   * @param context The context to determine how the node should be printed.
   */
Method code:
642 |   private void addNonEmptyExpression(
643 |       Node n, Context context, boolean allowNonBlockChild) {
644 |     Node nodeToProcess = n;
645 | 
646 |     if (!allowNonBlockChild && n.getType() != Token.BLOCK) {
647 |       throw new Error("Missing BLOCK child.");
648 |     }
649 | 
650 |     // Strip unneeded blocks, that is blocks with <2 children unless
651 |     // the CodePrinter specifically wants to keep them.
652 |     if (n.getType() == Token.BLOCK ) {
653 |       int count = getNonEmptyChildCount(n);
654 |       if (count == 0) {
655 |         if (cc.shouldPreserveExtraBlocks()) {
656 |           cc.beginBlock();
657 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
658 |         } else {
659 |           cc.endStatement(true);
660 |         }
661 |         return;
662 |       }
663 | 
664 |       if (count == 1) {
665 |         // Hack around a couple of browser bugs:
666 |         //   Safari needs a block around function declarations.
667 |         //   IE6/7 needs a block around DOs.
668 |         Node firstAndOnlyChild = getFirstNonEmptyChild(n);
669 |         boolean alwaysWrapInBlock = cc.shouldPreserveExtraBlocks();
670 |         if (alwaysWrapInBlock ||
671 |             firstAndOnlyChild.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
672 |             firstAndOnlyChild.getType() == Token.DO) {
673 |           cc.beginBlock();
674 |           add(firstAndOnlyChild, Context.STATEMENT);
675 |           cc.maybeLineBreak();
676 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
677 |           return;
678 |         } else {
679 |           // Continue with the only child.
680 |           nodeToProcess = firstAndOnlyChild;
681 |         }
682 |       }
683 |     }
684 | 
685 |     if (nodeToProcess.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
686 |       cc.endStatement(true);
687 |     } else {
688 |       add(nodeToProcess, context);
689 | 
690 |       // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions - so any
691 |       // VAR in a statement context needs a call to endStatement() here.
692 |       if (nodeToProcess.getType() == Token.VAR) {
693 |         cc.endStatement();
694 |       }
695 |     }
696 |   }

===== M0113 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineVariables.InliningBehavior.doInlinesForScope(NodeTraversal,Map<Var, ReferenceCollection>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineVariables.java
Original range: 196-224
Documentation:
/**
     * For all variables in this scope, see if they are only used once.
     * If it looks safe to do so, inline them.
     */
Method code:
196 |     private void doInlinesForScope(NodeTraversal t,
197 |         Map<Var, ReferenceCollection> referenceMap) {
198 | 
199 |       for (Iterator<Var> it = t.getScope().getVars(); it.hasNext();) {
200 |         Var v = it.next();
201 | 
202 |         ReferenceCollection referenceInfo = referenceMap.get(v);
203 | 
204 |         // referenceInfo will be null if we're in constants-only mode
205 |         // and the variable is not a constant.
206 |         if (referenceInfo == null || isVarInlineForbidden(v) ||
207 |             staleVars.contains(v)) {
208 |           // Never try to inline exported variables or variables that
209 |           // were not collected or variables that have already been inlined.
210 |           continue;
211 |         } else if (isInlineableDeclaredConstant(v, referenceInfo)) {
212 |           Reference init = referenceInfo.getInitializingReferenceForConstants();
213 |           Node value = init.getAssignedValue();
214 |           inlineDeclaredConstant(v, value, referenceInfo.references);
215 |           staleVars.add(v);
216 |         } else if (mode == Mode.CONSTANTS_ONLY) {
217 |           // If we're in constants-only mode, don't run more aggressive
218 |           // inlining heuristics. See InlineConstantsTest.
219 |           continue;
220 |         } else {
221 |           inlineNonConstants(v, referenceInfo);
222 |         }
223 |       }
224 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ReferenceCollectingCallback.ReferenceCollection.getInitializingReference()

/**
     * @return The reference that provides the value for the variable at the
     * time of the first read, if known, otherwise null.
     *
     * This is either the variable declaration ("var a = ...") or first
     * reference following the declaration if it is an assignment.

Method code:

===== M0120 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ReferenceCollectingCallback.ReferenceCollection.getOneAndOnlyAssignment()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ReferenceCollectingCallback.java
Original range: 369-383
Documentation:
/**
     * @return The one and only assignment. Returns if there are 0 or 2+
     *    assignments.
     */
Method code:
369 |     private Reference getOneAndOnlyAssignment() {
370 |       Reference assignment = null;
371 |       int size = references.size();
372 |       for (int i = 0; i < size; i++) {
373 |         Reference ref = references.get(i);
374 |         if (ref.isLvalue() || ref.isInitializingDeclaration()) {
375 |           if (assignment == null) {
376 |             assignment = ref;
377 |           } else {
378 |             return null;
379 |           }
380 |         }
381 |       }
382 |       return assignment;
383 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transformBlock(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 169-182
Documentation:

Method code:
169 |   private Node transformBlock(AstNode node) {
170 |     Node irNode = transform(node);
171 |     if (irNode.getType() != Token.BLOCK) {
172 |       if (irNode.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
173 |         irNode.setType(Token.BLOCK);
174 |         irNode.setWasEmptyNode(true);
175 |       } else {
176 |         Node newBlock = new Node(Token.BLOCK, irNode,
177 |             irNode.getLineno(), irNode.getCharno());
178 |         irNode = newBlock;
179 |       }
180 |     }
181 |     return irNode;
182 |   }

===== M0138 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 745-762
Documentation:

Method code:
745 |   public void removeProp(int propType) {
746 |     PropListItem x = propListHead;
747 |     if (x != null) {
748 |       PropListItem prev = null;
749 |       while (x.type != propType) {
750 |         prev = x;
751 |         x = x.next;
752 |         if (x == null) {
753 |           return;
754 |         }
755 |       }
756 |       if (prev == null) {
757 |         propListHead = x.next;
758 |       } else {
759 |         prev.next = x.next;
760 |       }
761 |     }
762 |   }

===== M0144 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeLineBreak()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 480-500
Documentation:

Method code:
480 |     @Override
481 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
482 |       if (lineBreak) {
483 |         if (sawFunction) {
484 |           startNewLine();
485 |           sawFunction = false;
486 |         }
487 |       }
488 | 
489 |       // Since we are at a legal line break, can we upgrade the
490 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a
491 |       // semicolon rather than before it.
492 |       int len = code.length();
493 |       if (preferredBreakPosition == len - 1) {
494 |         char ch = code.charAt(len - 1);
495 |         if (ch == ';') {
496 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
497 |         }
498 |       }
499 |       maybeCutLine();
500 |     }

1029 |   private static void toStringTreeHelper(Node n, int level, Appendable sb)
1030 |       throws IOException {

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Token.name(int)

Documentation:

Method code:
270 |   public static String name(int token)
271 |     {
272 |         if (!printNames) {
273 |             return String.valueOf(token);
274 |         }
275 |         switch (token) {
276 |           case ERROR:           return "ERROR";
277 |           case EOF:             return "EOF";
278 |           case EOL:             return "EOL";
279 |           case ENTERWITH:       return "ENTERWITH";
280 |           case LEAVEWITH:       return "LEAVEWITH";
281 |           case RETURN:          return "RETURN";
282 |           case GOTO:            return "GOTO";
283 |           case IFEQ:            return "IFEQ";
284 |           case IFNE:            return "IFNE";
285 |           case SETNAME:         return "SETNAME";
286 |           case BITOR:           return "BITOR";
287 |           case BITXOR:          return "BITXOR";
288 |           case BITAND:          return "BITAND";
289 |           case EQ:              return "EQ";
290 |           case NE:              return "NE";
291 |           case LT:              return "LT";
292 |           case LE:              return "LE";
293 |           case GT:              return "GT";
294 |           case GE:              return "GE";

===== M0178 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 176-197
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is an immutable value.
   */
Method code:
176 |   static boolean isImmutableValue(Node n) {
177 |     switch (n.getType()) {
178 |       case Token.STRING:
179 |       case Token.NUMBER:
180 |       case Token.NULL:
181 |       case Token.TRUE:
182 |       case Token.FALSE:
183 |       case Token.VOID:
184 |         return true;
185 |       case Token.NEG:
186 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
187 |       case Token.NAME:
188 |         String name = n.getString();
189 |         // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the keyword
190 |         // undefined to something other than the value undefined.
191 |         return "undefined".equals(name)
192 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
193 |             || "NaN".equals(name);
194 |     }
195 | 
196 |     return false;
197 |   }

===== M0183 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 105-139
Documentation:

Method code:
105 |   @Override
106 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
107 | 
108 |     switch (n.getType()) {
109 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
110 |         {
111 |           // Add recursive function name, if needed.
112 |           // NOTE: "enterScope" is called after we need to pick up this name.
113 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
114 | 
115 |           // If needed, add the function recursive name.
116 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
117 |           if (name != null && !name.isEmpty() && parent != null
118 |               && !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
119 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
120 |           }
121 | 
122 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
123 |         }
124 |         break;
125 | 
126 |       case Token.CATCH:
127 |         {
128 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
129 | 
130 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
131 |           renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
132 | 
133 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
134 |         }
135 |         break;
136 |     }
137 | 
138 |     return true;
139 |   }

===== M0194 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineVariables.InliningBehavior.isVarInlineForbidden(Var)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineVariables.java
Original range: 311-321
Documentation:
/**
     * Whether the given variable is forbidden from being inlined.
     */
Method code:
311 |     private boolean isVarInlineForbidden(Var var) {
312 |       // A variable may not be inlined if:
313 |       // 1) The variable is exported,
314 |       // 2) A reference to the variable has been inlined. We're downstream
315 |       //    of the mechanism that creates variable references, so we don't
316 |       //    have a good way to update the reference. Just punt on it.
317 |       // 3) Don't inline the special RENAME_PROPERTY_FUNCTION_NAME
318 |       return compiler.getCodingConvention().isExported(var.name)
319 |           || RenameProperties.RENAME_PROPERTY_FUNCTION_NAME.equals(var.name)
320 |           || staleVars.contains(var);
321 |     }

===== M0200 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 441-478
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
441 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
442 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
443 |     int type = n.getType();
444 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
445 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
446 |     }
447 | 
448 |     curNode = n;
449 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
450 | 
451 |     switch (type) {
452 |       case Token.CATCH:
453 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
454 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME);
455 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
456 |         // is the code block
457 |         traverseBranch(n.getFirstChild(), n);
458 |         traverseBranch(n.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext(), n);
459 |         break;
460 | 
461 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
462 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
463 |         break;
464 | 
465 |       default:
466 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
467 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
468 |           // would no longer point to the true next
469 |           Node next = child.getNext();
470 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
471 |           child = next;
472 |         }
473 |         break;
474 |     }
475 | 
476 |     curNode = n;
477 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
478 |   }

/**
   * @return Whether the node is used as a statement.

Method code:
1048 |   static boolean isStatement(Node n) {
1049 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
1050 |     // It is not possible to determine definitely if a node is a statement
1051 |     // or not if it is not part of the AST.  A FUNCTION node can be
1052 |     // either part of an expression or a statement.

===== M0206 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1564-1601
Documentation:
/** Returns true if this node is equivalent semantically to another */
Method code:
1564 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node) {
1565 |     if (type == Token.ARRAYLIT) {
1566 |       try {
1567 |         int[] indices1 = (int[]) getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1568 |         int[] indices2 = (int[]) node.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1569 |         if (indices1 == null) {
1570 |           if (indices2 != null) {
1571 |             return false;
1572 |           }
1573 |         } else if (indices2 == null) {
1574 |           return false;
1575 |         } else if (indices1.length != indices2.length) {
1576 |           return false;
1577 |         } else {
1578 |           for (int i = 0; i < indices1.length; i++) {
1579 |             if (indices1[i] != indices2[i]) {
1580 |               return false;
1581 |             }
1582 |           }
1583 |         }
1584 |       } catch (Exception e) {
1585 |         return false;
1586 |       }
1587 |     } else if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1588 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP, 0);
1589 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP, 0);
1590 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1591 |         return false;
1592 |       }
1593 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1594 |       int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP, 0);
1595 |       int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP, 0);
1596 |       if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1597 |         return false;
1598 |       }
1599 |     }
1600 |     return true;
1601 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 568-582
Documentation:
/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
568 |   public Scope getScope() {
569 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
570 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
571 |       return scope;
572 |     }
573 | 
574 |     Iterator<Node> it = scopeRoots.descendingIterator();
575 |     while (it.hasNext()) {
576 |       scope = scopeCreator.createScope(it.next(), scope);
577 |       scopes.push(scope);
578 |     }
579 |     scopeRoots.clear();
580 | 
581 |     return scope;
582 |   }

===== M0220 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 297-305
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
297 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
298 |       Node ret = transform(n);
299 |       if (ret.getType() == Token.STRING) {
300 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
301 |       } else if (ret.getType() == Token.NAME) {
302 |         ret.setType(Token.STRING);
303 |       }
304 |       return ret;
305 |     }

===== M0234 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 87-113
Documentation:

Method code:
87 |   @Override
88 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
89 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root,
90 |         new NormalizeStatements(compiler, assertOnChange));
91 |     if (MAKE_LOCAL_NAMES_UNIQUE) {
92 |       MakeDeclaredNamesUnique renamer = new MakeDeclaredNamesUnique();
93 |       NodeTraversal t = new NodeTraversal(compiler, renamer);
94 |       t.traverseRoots(externs, root);
95 |     }
96 |     // It is important that removeDuplicateDeclarations runs after
97 |     // MakeDeclaredNamesUnique in order for catch block exception names to be
98 |     // handled properly. Specifically, catch block exception names are
99 |     // only valid within the catch block, but our currect Scope logic
100 |     // has no concept of this and includes it in the containing function
101 |     // (or global scope). MakeDeclaredNamesUnique makes the catch exception
102 |     // names unique so that removeDuplicateDeclarations() will properly handle
103 |     // cases where a function scope variable conflict with a exception name:
104 |     //   function f() {
105 |     //      try {throw 0;} catch(e) {e; /* catch scope 'e'*/}
106 |     //      var e = 1; // f scope 'e'
107 |     //   }
108 |     // otherwise 'var e = 1' would be rewritten as 'e = 1'.
109 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Introduce a seperate scope for catch nodes.
110 |     removeDuplicateDeclarations(root);
111 |     new PropogateConstantAnnotations(compiler, assertOnChange)
112 |         .process(externs, root);
113 |   }

Method code:
85 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler, String sourceName,
86 |       String sourceStr) {
87 |     try {
88 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceName);
89 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceName, sourceStr,
90 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
91 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
92 |           logger_);
93 |     } catch (IOException e) {
94 |       compiler.report(JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceName));
95 |     }

98 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
99 |       root = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
100 |     } else {
101 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
102 |     }
103 |

105 |     // the source file and module.
106 |     root.putProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP, sourceName);
107 |   }

Method code:
251 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
252 |     this.type = type;
253 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
254 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();

256 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {

261 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
262 |         i++;

274 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {

278 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
279 |       trace.truncateEvents();

281 |

/**
   * Returns true if a name node represents a constant variable.
   *
   * <p>Determining whether a variable is constant has three steps:
   * <ol>
   * <li>In CodingConventionAnnotator, any name that matches the
   *     {@link CodingConvention#isConstant(String)} is annotated with an
   *     IS_CONSTANT_NAME property.
   * <li>The normalize pass renames any variable with the IS_CONSTANT_NAME
   *     annotation and that is initialized to a constant value with
   *     a variable name inlucding $$constant.

   * </ol>

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 262-288
Documentation:

Method code:
262 |   void addNumber(double x) {
263 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
264 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
265 |     char prev = getLastChar();
266 |     if (x < 0 && prev == '-') {
267 |       add(" ");
268 |     }
269 | 
270 |     if ((long) x == x) {
271 |       long value = (long) x;
272 |       long mantissa = value;
273 |       int exp = 0;
274 |       if (x >= 100) {
275 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
276 |           mantissa /= 10;
277 |           exp++;
278 |         }
279 |       }
280 |       if (exp > 2) {
281 |         add(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
282 |       } else {
283 |         add(Long.toString(value));
284 |       }
285 |     } else {
286 |       add(String.valueOf(x));
287 |     }
288 |   }

===== M0261 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,ObjectType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 587-594
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
587 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, ObjectType owner) {
588 |     Set<ObjectType> typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
589 |     if (typeSet == null) {
590 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet = Sets.newHashSet());
591 |     }
592 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
593 |     typeSet.add(owner);
594 |   }

===== M0279 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 59-81
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
59 |   @Override
60 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
61 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
62 |       return false;
63 |     }
64 | 
65 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
66 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
67 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
68 |     if (pos >= 0) {
69 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
70 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
71 |         return false;
72 |       }
73 |     }
74 | 
75 |     if (!Character.isUpperCase(name.charAt(0))) {
76 |       return false;
77 |     }
78 | 
79 |     // hack way of checking that there aren't any lower-case letters
80 |     return name.toUpperCase().equals(name);
81 |   }

===== M0290 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 232-260
Documentation:

Method code:
232 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
233 |     maybeEndStatement();
234 | 
235 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
236 |     char prev = getLastChar();
237 | 
238 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
239 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
240 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
241 |       append(" ");
242 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
243 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
244 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
245 |       append(" ");
246 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
247 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
248 |       append(" ");
249 |     }
250 | 
251 |     // Allow formating around the operator.
252 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
253 | 
254 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
255 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
256 |     // because it looks strange.
257 |     if (binOp) {
258 |       maybeCutLine();
259 |     }
260 |   }

===== M0293 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.splitVarDeclarations(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 455-473
Documentation:
/**
     * Split a var node such as:
     *   var a, b;
     * into individual statements:
     *   var a;
     *   var b;
     * @param n The whose children we should inspect.
     */
Method code:
455 |     private void splitVarDeclarations(Node n) {
456 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
457 |         next = c.getNext();
458 |         if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
459 |           if (assertOnChange && !c.hasChildren()) {
460 |             throw new IllegalStateException("Empty VAR node.");
461 |           }
462 | 
463 |           while (c.getFirstChild() != c.getLastChild()) {
464 |             Node name = c.getFirstChild();
465 |             c.removeChild(name);
466 |             Node newVar = new Node(
467 |                 Token.VAR, name, n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
468 |             n.addChildBefore(newVar, c);
469 |             reportCodeChange("VAR with multiple children");
470 |           }
471 |         }
472 |       }
473 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionParamBuilder.java
Original range: 94-109
Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

===== M0305 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 815-842
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
815 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
816 |     // unknown
817 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
818 |       return true;
819 |     }
820 |     // equality
821 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
822 |       return true;
823 |     }
824 |     // all type
825 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
826 |       return true;
827 |     }
828 |     // unions
829 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
830 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
831 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
832 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
833 |           return true;
834 |         }
835 |       }
836 |     }
837 |     // named types
838 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
839 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).referencedType);
840 |     }
841 |     return false;
842 |   }

===== M0308 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.moveNamedFunctions(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 479-511
Documentation:
/**
     * Move all the functions that are valid at the execution of the first
     * statement of the function to the beginning of the function definition.
     */
Method code:
479 |     private void moveNamedFunctions(Node functionBody) {
480 |       Preconditions.checkState(
481 |           functionBody.getParent().getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
482 |       Node previous = null;
483 |       Node current = functionBody.getFirstChild();
484 |       // Skip any declarations at the beginning of the function body, they
485 |       // are already in the right place.
486 |       while (current != null && NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
487 |         previous = current;
488 |         current = current.getNext();
489 |       }
490 | 
491 |       // Find any remaining declarations and move them.
492 |       Node insertAfter = previous;
493 |       while (current != null) {
494 |         // Save off the next node as the current node maybe removed.
495 |         Node next = current.getNext();
496 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
497 |           // Remove the declaration from the body.
498 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(previous);
499 |           functionBody.removeChildAfter(previous);
500 | 
501 |           // Readd the function at the top of the function body (after any
502 |           // previous declarations).
503 |           insertAfter = addToFront(functionBody, current, insertAfter);
504 |           reportCodeChange("Move function declaration not at top of function");
505 |         } else {
506 |           // Update the previous only if the current node hasn't been moved.
507 |           previous = current;
508 |         }
509 |         current = next;
510 |       }
511 |     }

   * Declares a variable.

380 |   Var declare(String name, Node nameNode,

===== M0321 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 904-924
Documentation:

Method code:
904 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
905 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
906 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
907 |       return s;
908 |     }
909 | 
910 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
911 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
912 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
913 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
914 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
915 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
916 |       // crazy.
917 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
918 |         sb.append(c);
919 |       } else {
920 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
921 |       }
922 |     }
923 |     return sb.toString();
924 |   }

===== M0325 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1373-1396
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1373 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1374 |     boolean eq = false;
1375 | 
1376 |     if (type == node2.getType() && getChildCount() == node2.getChildCount()
1377 |         && getNodeClass(this) == getNodeClass(node2)) {
1378 |       eq = this.isEquivalentTo(node2);
1379 |     }
1380 | 
1381 |     if (!eq) {
1382 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1383 |     }
1384 | 
1385 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1386 |     Node n, n2;
1387 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1388 |          res == null && n != null;
1389 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1390 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1391 |       if (res != null) {
1392 |         return res;
1393 |       }
1394 |     }
1395 |     return res;
1396 |   }

     * make sense to use a ListResourceBundle instead of a properties
     * file to avoid (synchronized) text parsing.
     */
Method code:

464 | 
465 |         // ResourceBundle does cacheing.
466 |         ResourceBundle rb = ResourceBundle.getBundle(defaultResource, locale);
467 | 
468 |         String formatString;
469 |         try {
470 |             formatString = rb.getString(messageId);
471 |         } catch (java.util.MissingResourceException mre) {
472 |             throw new RuntimeException
473 |                 ("no message resource found for message property "+ messageId);
474 |         }
475 | 
476 |         /*
477 |          * It's OK to format the string, even if 'arguments' is null;
478 |          * we need to format it anyway, to make double ''s collapse to
479 |          * single 's.
480 |          */
481 |         // TODO: MessageFormat is not available on pJava
482 |         MessageFormat formatter = new MessageFormat(formatString);
483 |         return formatter.format(arguments);
484 |     }

/**
   * Creates a union.
   * @return A UnionType if it has two or more alternates, the
   *    only alternate if it has one and otherwise {@code NO_TYPE}.
   */
Method code:
136 |   JSType build() {
137 |     if (result == null) {
138 |       if (isAllType) {
139 |         result = registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
140 |       } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
141 |         if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
142 |           result = registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);

144 |           result = registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
145 |         }

148 |         int size = alternateSet.size();
149 |         if (size > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
150 |           result = registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
151 |         } else {
152 |           if (size > 1) {

154 |           } else if (size == 1) {

156 |           } else {

159 |         }

===== M0341 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 85-129
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
85 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
86 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
87 |     // just bail out early here.
88 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
89 |       return this;
90 |     }
91 | 
92 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
93 | 
94 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
95 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
96 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
97 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
98 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
99 |     }
100 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
101 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
102 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
103 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
104 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
105 |         }
106 |       } else {
107 |         if (!alternate.isUnknownType()) {
108 |           Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
109 |           while (it.hasNext()) {
110 |             JSType current = it.next();
111 |             if (!current.isUnknownType()) {
112 |               if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
113 |                 // Alternate is unnecessary.
114 |                 return this;
115 |               } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
116 |                 // Alternate makes current obsolete
117 |                 it.remove();
118 |               }
119 |             }
120 |           }
121 |         }
122 |         alternates.add(alternate);
123 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
124 |       }
125 |     } else {
126 |       result = null;
127 |     }
128 |     return this;
129 |   }

/**
     * If any of the variables are well-defined and alias other variables,
     * mark them as aliasing candidates.
     */
Method code:
169 |     private void collectAliasCandidates(NodeTraversal t,
170 |         Map<Var, ReferenceCollection> referenceMap) {
171 |       if (mode != Mode.CONSTANTS_ONLY) {
172 |         for (Iterator<Var> it = t.getScope().getVars(); it.hasNext();) {
173 |           Var v = it.next();

176 |           // NOTE(nicksantos): Don't handle variables that are never used.
177 |           // The tests are much easier to write if you don't, and there's

179 |           if (referenceInfo != null && referenceInfo.references.size() >= 2 &&

===== M0363 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 892-1008
Documentation:

Method code:
892 |   private void toString(
893 |       StringBuilder sb,
894 |       boolean printSource,
895 |       boolean printAnnotations,
896 |       boolean printType) {
897 |     if (Token.printTrees) {
898 |       sb.append(Token.name(type));
899 |       if (this instanceof StringNode) {
900 |         sb.append(' ');
901 |         sb.append(getString());
902 |       } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
903 |         sb.append(' ');
904 |         // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
905 |         // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
906 |         // toStringTree.
907 |         if (first.getType() == Token.STRING) {
908 |           sb.append(first.getString());
909 |         }
910 |       } else if (this instanceof ScriptOrFnNode) {
911 |         ScriptOrFnNode sof = (ScriptOrFnNode) this;
912 |         if (this instanceof FunctionNode) {
913 |           FunctionNode fn = (FunctionNode) this;
914 |           sb.append(' ');
915 |           sb.append(fn.getFunctionName());
916 |         }
917 |         if (printSource) {
918 |           sb.append(" [source name: ");
919 |           sb.append(sof.getSourceName());
920 |           sb.append("] [encoded source length: ");
921 |           sb.append(sof.getEncodedSourceEnd() - sof.getEncodedSourceStart());
922 |           sb.append("] [base line: ");
923 |           sb.append(sof.getBaseLineno());
924 |           sb.append("] [end line: ");
925 |           sb.append(sof.getEndLineno());
926 |           sb.append(']');
927 |         }
928 |       } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
929 |         sb.append(' ');
930 |         sb.append(getDouble());
931 |       }
932 |       if (printSource) {
933 |         int lineno = getLineno();
934 |         if (lineno != -1) {
935 |           sb.append(' ');
936 |           sb.append(lineno);
937 |         }
938 |       }
939 | 
940 |       if (printAnnotations) {
941 |         int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
942 |         for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
943 |           int type = keys[i];
944 |           PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
945 |           sb.append(" [");
946 |           sb.append(propToString(type));
947 |           sb.append(": ");
948 |           String value;
949 |           switch (type) {
950 |             case TARGETBLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it recurses
951 |               value = "target block property";
952 |               break;
953 |             case LOCAL_BLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it is dull
954 |               value = "last local block";
955 |               break;
956 |             case ISNUMBER_PROP:
957 |               switch (x.intValue) {
958 |                 case BOTH:
959 |                   value = "both";
960 |                   break;
961 |                 case RIGHT:
962 |                   value = "right";
963 |                   break;
964 |                 case LEFT:
965 |                   value = "left";
966 |                   break;
967 |                 default:
968 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
969 |               }
970 |               break;
971 |             case SPECIALCALL_PROP:
972 |               switch (x.intValue) {
973 |                 case SPECIALCALL_EVAL:
974 |                   value = "eval";
975 |                   break;
976 |                 case SPECIALCALL_WITH:
977 |                   value = "with";
978 |                   break;
979 |                 default:
980 |                   // NON_SPECIALCALL should not be stored
981 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
982 |               }
983 |               break;
984 |             default:
985 |               Object obj = x.objectValue;
986 |               if (obj != null) {
987 |                 value = obj.toString();
988 |               } else {
989 |                 value = String.valueOf(x.intValue);
990 |               }
991 |               break;
992 |           }
993 |           sb.append(value);
994 |           sb.append(']');
995 |         }
996 |       }
997 | 
998 |       if (printType) {
999 |         if (jsType != null) {
1000 |           String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
1001 |           if (jsTypeString != null) {
1002 |             sb.append(" : ");
1003 |             sb.append(jsTypeString);
1004 |           }
1005 |         }
1006 |       }
1007 |     }
1008 |   }

===== M0366 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1232-1234
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1232 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1233 |     return n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION && !isStatement(n);
1234 |   }
