===== M0368 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 406-450
Documentation:

Method code:
406 |   @Override
407 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
408 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
409 |       return true;
410 |     }
411 | 
412 |     // Union types
413 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
414 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
415 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
416 |       return false;
417 |     }
418 | 
419 |     // record types
420 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
421 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
422 |     }
423 | 
424 |     // Interfaces
425 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
426 |     // to the interface instance.
427 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
428 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
429 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
430 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
431 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
432 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
433 |           return true;
434 |         }
435 |       }
436 |     }
437 | 
438 |     // other prototype based objects
439 |     if (that != null) {
440 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
441 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
442 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
443 |         // to avoid guessing.
444 |         return true;
445 |       }
446 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     return false;
450 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ReferenceCollectingCallback.BasicBlock.provablyExecutesBefore(BasicBlock)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ReferenceCollectingCallback.java
Original range: 572-585
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines whether this block is guaranteed to begin executing before
     * the given block does.
     */
Method code:
572 |     boolean provablyExecutesBefore(BasicBlock thatBlock) {
573 |       // If thatBlock is a descendant of this block, and there are no hoisted
574 |       // blocks between them, then this block must start before thatBlock.
575 |       BasicBlock currentBlock;
576 |       for (currentBlock = thatBlock;
577 |            currentBlock != null && currentBlock != this;
578 |            currentBlock = currentBlock.getParent()) {
579 |         if (currentBlock.isHoisted) {
580 |           return false;
581 |         }
582 |       }
583 | 
584 |       return currentBlock == this;
585 |     }

===== M0404 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.enterScope(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 64-96
Documentation:

Method code:
64 |   @Override
65 |   public void enterScope(NodeTraversal t) {
66 |     Node declarationRoot = t.getScopeRoot();
67 |     Renamer renamer;
68 |     if (nameStack.isEmpty()) {
69 |       // If the contextual renamer is being used the starting context can not
70 |       // be a function.
71 |       Preconditions.checkState(
72 |           declarationRoot.getType() != Token.FUNCTION ||
73 |           !(rootRenamer instanceof ContextualRenamer));
74 |       Preconditions.checkState(t.inGlobalScope());
75 |       renamer = rootRenamer;
76 |     } else {
77 |       renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
78 |     }
79 | 
80 |     if (declarationRoot.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
81 |       // Add the function parameters
82 |       Node fnParams = declarationRoot.getFirstChild().getNext();
83 |       for (Node c = fnParams.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
84 |         String name = c.getString();
85 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
86 |       }
87 | 
88 |       // Add the function body declarations
89 |       Node functionBody = declarationRoot.getLastChild();
90 |       findDeclaredNames(functionBody, null, renamer);
91 |     } else {
92 |       // Add the block declarations
93 |       findDeclaredNames(declarationRoot, null, renamer);
94 |     }
95 |     nameStack.push(renamer);
96 |   }

===== M0405 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineVariables.InliningBehavior.canInline(Reference,Reference,Reference)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineVariables.java
Original range: 487-544
Documentation:
/**
     * @return true if the provided reference and declaration can be safely
     *         inlined according to our criteria
     */
Method code:
487 |     private boolean canInline(
488 |         Reference declaration,
489 |         Reference initialization,
490 |         Reference reference) {
491 |       if (!isValidDeclaration(declaration)
492 |           || !isValidInitialization(initialization)
493 |           || !isValidReference(reference)) {
494 |         return false;
495 |       }
496 | 
497 |       // If the value is read more than once, skip it.
498 |       // VAR declarations and EXPR_RESULT don't need the value, but other
499 |       // ASSIGN expressions parents do.
500 |       if (declaration != initialization &&
501 |           initialization.getGrandparent().getType() != Token.EXPR_RESULT) {
502 |         return false;
503 |       }
504 | 
505 |       // Be very conservative and do no cross control structures or
506 |       // scope boundaries
507 |       if (declaration.getBasicBlock() != initialization.getBasicBlock()
508 |           || declaration.getBasicBlock() != reference.getBasicBlock()) {
509 |         return false;
510 |       }
511 | 
512 |       // Do not inline into a call node. This would change
513 |       // the context in which it was being called. For example,
514 |       //   var a = b.c;
515 |       //   a();
516 |       // should not be inlined, because it calls a in the context of b
517 |       // rather than the context of the window.
518 |       //   var a = b.c;
519 |       //   f(a)
520 |       // is ok.
521 |       Node value = initialization.getAssignedValue();
522 |       Preconditions.checkState(value != null);
523 |       if (value.getType() == Token.GETPROP
524 |           && reference.getParent().getType() == Token.CALL
525 |           && reference.getParent().getFirstChild() == reference.getNameNode()) {
526 |         return false;
527 |       }
528 | 
529 |       // Bug 2388531: Don't inline subclass definitions into class defining
530 |       // calls as this confused class removing logic.
531 |       if (value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
532 |         Node callNode = reference.getParent();
533 |         if (reference.getParent().getType() == Token.CALL) {
534 |           SubclassRelationship relationship =
535 |               compiler.getCodingConvention().getClassesDefinedByCall(callNode);
536 |           if (relationship != null) {
537 |             return false;
538 |           }
539 |         }
540 |       }
541 | 
542 |       return canMoveAggressively(value) ||
543 |           canMoveModerately(initialization, reference);
544 |     }

===== M0406 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)

Method code:
511 |    @Override

518 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
519 |       }
520 |       Node node = new Node(Token.FUNCTION);
521 |       node.putProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP, functionNode.getSourceName());
522 |       Node newName = transform(name);
523 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
524 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
525 |         // declaration.
526 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
527 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
528 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
529 |         // same line as the function keyword.
530 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
531 |             functionNode.getLp();
532 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
533 |       }
534 | 
535 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
536 |       Node lp = new Node(Token.LP);
537 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
538 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
539 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
540 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
541 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
542 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
543 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
544 |       if (fnName != null) {
545 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
546 |       } else {
547 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
548 |       }
549 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
550 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
551 | 
552 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
553 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
554 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
555 |       }
556 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
557 | 
558 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
559 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
560 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
561 |      return node;
562 |     }

===== M0417 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineVariables.InliningBehavior.inlineNonConstants(Var,ReferenceCollection)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineVariables.java
Original range: 226-292
Documentation:

Method code:
226 |     private void inlineNonConstants(
227 |         Var v, ReferenceCollection referenceInfo) {
228 |       int refCount = referenceInfo.references.size();
229 |       Reference declaration = referenceInfo.references.get(0);
230 |       Reference init = referenceInfo.getInitializingReference();
231 |       int firstRefAfterInit = (declaration == init) ? 2 : 3;
232 | 
233 |       if (refCount > 1 &&
234 |           isImmutableAndWellDefinedVariable(v, referenceInfo)) {
235 |         // if the variable is referenced more than once, we can only
236 |         // inline it if it's immutable and never defined before referenced.
237 |         Node value;
238 |         if (init != null) {
239 |           value = init.getAssignedValue();
240 |         } else {
241 |           // Create a new node for variable that is never initialized.
242 |           Node srcLocation = declaration.getNameNode();
243 |           value = NodeUtil.newUndefinedNode(srcLocation);
244 |         }
245 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(value);
246 |         inlineWellDefinedVariable(v, value, referenceInfo.references);
247 |         staleVars.add(v);
248 |       } else if (refCount == firstRefAfterInit) {
249 |         // The variable likely only read once, try some more
250 |         // complex inlining heuristics.
251 |         Reference reference = referenceInfo.references.get(
252 |             firstRefAfterInit - 1);
253 |         if (canInline(declaration, init, reference)) {
254 |           inline(v, declaration, init, reference);
255 |           staleVars.add(v);
256 |         }
257 |       } else if (declaration != init && refCount == 2) {
258 |         if (isValidDeclaration(declaration) && isValidInitialization(init)) {
259 |           // The only reference is the initialization, remove the assignment and
260 |           // the variable declaration.
261 |           Node value = init.getAssignedValue();
262 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(value);
263 |           inlineWellDefinedVariable(v, value, referenceInfo.references);
264 |           staleVars.add(v);
265 |         }
266 |       }
267 | 
268 |       // If this variable was not inlined normally, check if we can
269 |       // inline an alias of it. (If the variable was inlined, then the
270 |       // reference data is out of sync. We're better off just waiting for
271 |       // the next pass.)
272 |       if (!staleVars.contains(v) && referenceInfo.isWellDefined() &&
273 |           referenceInfo.isAssignedOnceInLifetime()) {
274 |         List<Reference> refs = referenceInfo.references;
275 |         for (int i = 1 /* start from a read */; i < refs.size(); i++) {
276 |           Node nameNode = refs.get(i).getNameNode();
277 |           if (aliasCandidates.containsKey(nameNode)) {
278 |             AliasCandidate candidate = aliasCandidates.get(nameNode);
279 |             if (!staleVars.contains(candidate.alias)) {
280 |               Reference aliasInit;
281 |               aliasInit = candidate.refInfo.getInitializingReference();
282 |               Node value = aliasInit.getAssignedValue();
283 |               Preconditions.checkNotNull(value);
284 |               inlineWellDefinedVariable(candidate.alias,
285 |                   value,
286 |                   candidate.refInfo.references);
287 |               staleVars.add(candidate.alias);
288 |             }
289 |           }
290 |         }
291 |       }
292 |     }

===== M0418 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(String,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 80-114
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
80 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
81 |                            String sourceString,
82 |                            Config config,
83 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
84 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
85 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
86 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
87 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
88 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
89 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
90 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
91 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
92 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(true);
93 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
94 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
95 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
96 |     }
97 | 
98 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
99 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
100 |     try {
101 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
102 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
103 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
104 |     } finally {
105 |       Context.exit();
106 |     }
107 |     Node root = null;
108 |     if (astRoot != null) {
109 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
110 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
111 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
112 |     }
113 |     return root;
114 |   }

===== M0442 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.extractForInitializer(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 411-445
Documentation:
/**
     * Bring the initializers out of FOR loops.  These need to be placed
     * before any associated LABEL nodes. This needs to be done from the top
     * level label first so this is called as a pre-order callback (from
     * shouldTraverse).
     *
     * @param n The node to inspect.
     * @param before The node to insert the initializer before.
     * @param beforeParent The parent of the node before which the initializer
     *     will be inserted.
     */
Method code:
411 |     private void extractForInitializer(
412 |         Node n, Node before, Node beforeParent) {
413 | 
414 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
415 |         next = c.getNext();
416 |         Node insertBefore = (before == null) ? c : before;
417 |         Node insertBeforeParent = (before == null) ? n : beforeParent;
418 |         switch (c.getType()) {
419 |           case Token.LABEL:
420 |             extractForInitializer(c, insertBefore, insertBeforeParent);
421 |             break;
422 |           case Token.FOR:
423 |             if (!NodeUtil.isForIn(c)
424 |                 && c.getFirstChild().getType() != Token.EMPTY) {
425 |               Node init = c.getFirstChild();
426 |               Node empty = new Node(Token.EMPTY);
427 |               empty.copyInformationFrom(c);
428 |               c.replaceChild(init, empty);
429 | 
430 |               Node newStatement;
431 |               // Only VAR statements, and expressions are allowed,
432 |               // but are handled differently.
433 |               if (init.getType() == Token.VAR) {
434 |                 newStatement = init;
435 |               } else {
436 |                 newStatement = NodeUtil.newExpr(init);
437 |               }
438 | 
439 |               insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
440 |               reportCodeChange("FOR initializer");
441 |             }

     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line

506 |     @Override
507 |     void maybeCutLine() {
508 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
509 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
510 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
511 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
512 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
513 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
514 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition);
515 |           lineIndex++;
516 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);

518 |         } else {
519 |           startNewLine();
520 |         }

===== M0459 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 657-732
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
657 |   static int precedence(int type) {
658 |     switch (type) {
659 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
660 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
661 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
662 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
663 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
664 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
665 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
666 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
667 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
668 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
669 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
670 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
671 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
672 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
673 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
674 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
675 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
676 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
677 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
678 |       case Token.EQ:
679 |       case Token.NE:
680 |       case Token.SHEQ:
681 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
682 |       case Token.LT:
683 |       case Token.GT:
684 |       case Token.LE:
685 |       case Token.GE:
686 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
687 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
688 |       case Token.LSH:
689 |       case Token.RSH:
690 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
691 |       case Token.SUB:
692 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
693 |       case Token.MUL:
694 |       case Token.MOD:
695 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
696 |       case Token.INC:
697 |       case Token.DEC:
698 |       case Token.NEW:
699 |       case Token.DELPROP:
700 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
701 |       case Token.VOID:
702 |       case Token.NOT:
703 |       case Token.BITNOT:
704 |       case Token.POS:
705 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
706 | 
707 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
708 |       case Token.CALL:
709 |       case Token.EMPTY:
710 |       case Token.FALSE:
711 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
712 |       case Token.GETELEM:
713 |       case Token.GETPROP:
714 |       case Token.GET_REF:
715 |       case Token.IF:
716 |       case Token.LP:
717 |       case Token.NAME:
718 |       case Token.NULL:
719 |       case Token.NUMBER:
720 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
721 |       case Token.REGEXP:
722 |       case Token.RETURN:
723 |       case Token.STRING:
724 |       case Token.THIS:
725 |       case Token.TRUE:
726 |         return 15;
727 | 
728 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
729 |                                Node.tokenToName(type) +
730 |                                " (type " + type + ")");
731 |     }
732 |   }

===== M0462 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.findDeclaredNames(Node,Node,Renamer)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 187-204
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses the current scope and collects declared names.  Does not
   * decent into functions or add CATCH exceptions.
   */
Method code:
187 |   private void findDeclaredNames(Node n, Node parent, Renamer renamer) {
188 |     // Do a shallow traversal, so don't traverse into function declarations,
189 |     // except for the name of the function itself.
190 |     if (parent == null
191 |         || parent.getType() != Token.FUNCTION
192 |         || n == parent.getFirstChild()) {
193 |       if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n)) {
194 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(n.getString());
195 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
196 |         Node nameNode = n.getFirstChild();
197 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(nameNode.getString());
198 |       }
199 | 
200 |       for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
201 |         findDeclaredNames(c, n, renamer);
202 |       }
203 |     }
204 |   }

===== M0465 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transform(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 184-214
Documentation:

Method code:
184 |   private Node transform(AstNode node) {
185 |     String jsDoc = node.getJsDoc();
186 |     NodeWithJsDoc nodeWithJsDoc = null;
187 |     if (jsDoc != null) {
188 |       nodeWithJsDoc = new NodeWithJsDoc();
189 |       nodesWithJsDoc.put(jsDoc, nodeWithJsDoc);
190 |     }
191 | 
192 |     Node irNode = justTransform(node);
193 |     if (nodeWithJsDoc != null) {
194 |       nodeWithJsDoc.node = irNode;
195 |     }
196 | 
197 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
198 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
199 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
200 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
201 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());
202 |     } else {
203 |       if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
204 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
205 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
206 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
207 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();
208 |         irNode.setLineno(lineno);
209 |         int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());
210 |         irNode.setCharno(charno);
211 |       }
212 |     }
213 |     return irNode;
214 |   }

===== M0468 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transformTree(AstRoot,String,Config,ErrorReporter)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 121-167
Documentation:

Method code:
121 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,
122 |                                    String sourceString,
123 |                                    Config config,
124 |                                    ErrorReporter errorReporter) {
125 |     IRFactory irFactory = new IRFactory(sourceString, node.getSourceName(),
126 |         config, errorReporter);
127 |     Node irNode = irFactory.transform(node);
128 |     // @license text gets appended onto the fileLevelJsDocBuilder as found,
129 |     // and stored straight into the JSDocInfo for the root node.
130 |     Node.FileLevelJsDocBuilder fileLevelJsDocBuilder =
131 |         irNode.getJsDocBuilderForNode();
132 |     // fileOverviewInfo stores the last bit of fileoverview data we saw.
133 |     // We only permit one, so throwing away extras is fair.
134 |     // The fileOverviewInfo gets passed into parseJSDocInfo so that
135 |     // it can detect when multiple @fileoverviews exist in the same file.
136 |     JSDocInfo fileOverviewInfo = null;
137 |     if (node.getComments() != null) {
138 |       for (Comment comment : node.getComments()) {
139 |         if (comment.getCommentType() == JSDOC) {
140 |           JsDocInfoParser jsDocParser =
141 |               irFactory.createJsDocInfoParser(comment.getValue(),
142 |                   comment.getLineno(), comment.getAbsolutePosition(),
143 |                   fileLevelJsDocBuilder, fileOverviewInfo);
144 |           if (jsDocParser.getFileOverviewJSDocInfo() != fileOverviewInfo) {
145 |             fileOverviewInfo = jsDocParser.getFileOverviewJSDocInfo();
146 |           } else {
147 |             JSDocInfo info = jsDocParser.retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
148 |             if (info != null) {
149 |               irFactory.attachJsDoc(comment, info);
150 |             }
151 |           }
152 |         }
153 |       }
154 | 
155 |       // Only after we've seen all @fileoverview entries, attach the
156 |       // last one to the root node, and copy the found license strings
157 |       // to that node.
158 |       if (fileOverviewInfo != null) {
159 |         if ((irNode.getJSDocInfo() != null) &&
160 |             (irNode.getJSDocInfo().getLicense() != null)) {
161 |           fileOverviewInfo.setLicense(irNode.getJSDocInfo().getLicense());
162 |         }
163 |         irNode.setJSDocInfo(fileOverviewInfo);
164 |       }
165 |     }
166 |     return irNode;
167 |   }

===== M0469 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineVariables.InliningBehavior.isValidInitialization(Reference)

Documentation:
/**
     * @return Whether there is a initial value.
     */
Method code:
606 |     private boolean isValidInitialization(Reference initialization) {
607 |       if (initialization == null) {
608 |         return false;
609 |       } else if (initialization.isDeclaration()) {
610 |         // The reference is a FUNCTION declaration or normal VAR declaration
611 |         // with a value.
612 |         return NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(initialization.getParent())
613 |             || initialization.getNameNode().getFirstChild() != null;
614 |       } else {

616 |         Preconditions.checkState(
617 |             parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN
618 |             && parent.getFirstChild() == initialization.getNameNode());
619 |         return true;
620 |       }
621 |     }

===== M0482 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 483-518
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
483 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
484 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
485 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
486 | 
487 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
488 | 
489 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
490 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
491 | 
492 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
493 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
494 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
495 |     }
496 | 
497 |     curNode = n;
498 |     pushScope(n);
499 | 
500 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
501 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
502 |       // scope.
503 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
504 |     }
505 | 
506 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
507 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
508 | 
509 |     // Args
510 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
511 | 
512 |     // Body
513 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
514 |             body.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
515 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
516 | 
517 |     popScope();
518 |   }

/**
   * Creates a Scope given the parent Scope and the root node of the scope.
   * @param parent  The parent Scope. Cannot be null.
   * @param rootNode  Typically the FUNCTION node.
   */
Method code:
282 |   Scope(Scope parent, Node rootNode) {
283 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(parent);
284 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(rootNode != parent.rootNode);
285 | 
286 |     this.parent = parent;
287 |     this.rootNode = rootNode;
288 |     JSType nodeType = rootNode.getJSType();
289 |     if (nodeType != null && nodeType instanceof FunctionType) {
290 |       thisType = ((FunctionType) nodeType).getTypeOfThis();
291 |     } else {
292 |       thisType = parent.thisType;
293 |     }
294 |     this.isBottom = false;
295 |   }

===== M0487 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createParameters(boolean,JSType...)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1074-1085
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1074 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1075 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1076 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1077 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1078 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1079 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1080 |       } else {
1081 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1082 |       }
1083 |     }
1084 |     return builder.build();
1085 |   }

963 |   static boolean isControlStructure(Node n) {
964 |     switch (n.getType()) {
965 |       case Token.FOR:
966 |       case Token.DO:
967 |       case Token.WHILE:
968 |       case Token.WITH:
969 |       case Token.IF:
970 |       case Token.LABEL:
971 |       case Token.TRY:
972 |       case Token.CATCH:
973 |       case Token.SWITCH:
974 |       case Token.CASE:
975 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
976 |         return true;
977 |       default:
978 |         return false;
979 |     }
980 |   }

===== M0504 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 368-384
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
368 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
369 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
370 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
371 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
372 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
373 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
374 |         String inputName = input.getName();
375 | 
376 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
377 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
378 |         // will get an error down the line.
379 |         inputs.add(input);
380 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
381 |       }
382 |     }
383 |     return inputs;
384 |   }

// Gets all the property types, in sorted order.
Method code:
811 |   private int[] getSortedPropTypes() {
812 |     int count = 0;
813 |     for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.next) {
814 |       count++;

===== M0525 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 141-167
Documentation:

Method code:
141 |   @Override
142 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
143 |     switch (n.getType()) {
144 |       case Token.NAME:
145 |         String newName = getReplacementName(n.getString());
146 |         if (newName != null) {
147 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek();
148 |           if (renamer.stripConstIfReplaced()) {
149 |             // TODO(johnlenz): Do we need to do anything about the javadoc?
150 |             n.removeProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
151 |           }
152 |           n.setString(newName);
153 |           t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
154 |         }
155 |         break;
156 | 
157 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
158 |         // Remove function recursive name (if any).
159 |         nameStack.pop();
160 |         break;
161 | 
162 |       case Token.CATCH:
163 |         // Remove catch except name from the stack of names.
164 |         nameStack.pop();
165 |         break;
166 |     }
167 |   }

===== M0529 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ErrorFunctionType.ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry,String)

Original range: 52-76

Method code:
52 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {
53 |     super(
54 |         registry, name, null,
55 |         registry.createArrowType(
56 |             registry.createOptionalParameters(
57 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
58 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),

===== M0530 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 84-114
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
85 |     if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
86 |       sourceName = (String) n.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
87 | 
88 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
89 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
90 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
91 | 
92 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
93 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
94 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
95 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
96 |         declareVar(fnName, fnNameNode, n, null, null, n);
97 |       }
98 | 
99 |       // Args: Declare function variables
100 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.getType() == Token.LP);
101 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
102 |            a = a.getNext()) {
103 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.getType() == Token.NAME);
104 |         declareVar(a.getString(), a, args, n, null, n);
105 |       }
106 | 
107 |       // Body
108 |       scanVars(body, n);
109 |     } else {
110 |       // It's the global block
111 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
112 |       scanVars(n, null);
113 |     }
114 |   }

452 |   private boolean implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown() {
453 |     ObjectType p = getImplicitPrototype();
454 |     while (p != null) {
455 |       if (p.isUnknownType()) {
456 |         return true;
457 |       }
458 |       p = p.getImplicitPrototype();
459 |     }
460 |     return false;
461 |   }

/**
   * Is this node a hoisted function declaration? A function declaration in the
   * scope root is hoisted to the top of the scope.
   * See {@link #isFunctionDeclaration}).
   */
Method code:
1203 |   static boolean isHoistedFunctionDeclaration(Node n) {
1204 |     return isFunctionDeclaration(n)
1205 |         && (n.getParent().getType() == Token.SCRIPT
1206 |             || n.getParent().getParent().getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
1207 |   }

===== M0542 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 119-175
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
119 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
120 |     switch (n.getType()) {
121 |       case Token.VAR:
122 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
123 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
124 |              child != null;) {
125 |           Node next = child.getNext();
126 |           Preconditions.checkState(child.getType() == Token.NAME);
127 | 
128 |           String name = child.getString();
129 |           declareVar(name, child, n, parent, null, n);
130 |           child = next;
131 |         }
132 |         return;
133 | 
134 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
135 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
136 |           return;
137 |         }
138 | 
139 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
140 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
141 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
142 |           return;
143 |         }
144 |         declareVar(fnName, n.getFirstChild(), n, parent, null, n);
145 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
146 | 
147 |       case Token.CATCH:
148 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
149 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME);
150 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
151 |         // is the code block
152 | 
153 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
154 |         final Node block = var.getNext().getNext();
155 | 
156 |         declareVar(var.getString(), var, n, parent, null, n);
157 |         scanVars(block, n);
158 |         return;  // only one child to scan
159 | 
160 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
161 |         sourceName = (String) n.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
162 |         break;
163 |     }
164 | 
165 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
166 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
167 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
168 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
169 |            child != null;) {
170 |         Node next = child.getNext();
171 |         scanVars(child, n);
172 |         child = next;
173 |       }
174 |     }
175 |   }

===== M0543 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 401-415
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
401 |   @Override
402 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
403 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
404 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
405 |     if (unknown) {
406 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
407 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
408 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
409 |         unknown = false;
410 |       } else {
411 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
412 |       }
413 |     }
414 |     return unknown;
415 |   }
